02線上報名表拆解

五個步驟，林翰主填。表內資料會直接用於大會出版物、官網與官方社群，填進去的就是對外版本。

狀態：8/3 已送出。Sujin 已代修技術欄（24fps SMPTE，原誤選 InterOP）並記錄僅 1 張劇照；關表後再改動要直接聯絡 Program Team。（還缺什麼見待確認清單）出處：信件 ✉7✉8
直接開啟：開啟線上報名表（已含影片編號）開啟官方範例存取碼 出處：信件 ✉1

表單為最新版 Chrome 最佳化；須由「適當的申請人」填寫（出品公司或其授權公司）；技術問題直接寄 filmguest@biff.kr。送出後任何變動都要直接回報 Program Team。✉1表單 Step 5

Step 1影片資訊 Film Info›

基本資料

  • 英文片名、原文片名、韓文用羅馬拼音（照發音拼寫，ISO 基本拉丁字母）
  • 完成日期（月／年，指影片實際完成的時間）
  • 語言、字幕語言、製作國（可加多國）、片長、類別（劇情／紀錄／動畫／其他）
  • AI 參與：無／部分／全部，有 AI 生成畫面就要勾
  • Premiere 欄：World / International / Asian / Korean 四選一文件明載已填 Asian Premiere（TIFF 9/17 世界首映在先），大會 8/4 複查表同列此項。✉8
  • 影展紀錄：BIFF 之前已放映或將放映的影展都要列，要列 TIFF 2026；得獎／補助如有也填
  • 短版大綱：45 字（words）以內；長版大綱另填，兩者長度必須不同，完全一樣會被要求重交

導演欄

  • 姓名拼寫規則：姓氏全大寫、名字首字母大寫（表單會擋，例：Fiona ROAN）
  • 國籍、性別、Email、電話
  • 導演照：jpg、300dpi 以上、高於 1080×1440 像素、單檔 10MB 內；有版權要標版權人、不加©符號
  • 簡歷：45 字（words）以上；作品年表逐年列

技術規格

  • 放映格式：無加密 DCP 最優先；加密 DCP 要另寄 DKDM 給大會 Print Coordinator
  • 2D/3D、彩色/黑白、2K/4K、FPS：DCP 只收 24 或 25 fps，其他格率無法放映
  • DCP 硬碟：3.5" HDD 或 USB 介面外接盒＋外接電源；MBR 分割表；NTFS／EXT2／EXT3
  • 字幕以燒入為佳；XML 字幕的字型檔須小於 640KB；聲音建議 5.1 聲道

四個授權決策（勾了就是同意）

  • 宣傳片段：上傳或給連結＋密碼；不給的話 BIFF 會自行從乾淨版 screener 剪 1–2 分鐘，想控制對外露出什麼，就自己給LINE
  • Festival Preview Service：影展開跑前，供事先登記核可的媒體受限試看全片（確切期間以正式表單原文為準）
  • Festival Video Library：影展期間限場內供專業人士看片、不得複製外借；影展後 screener 永久存於釜山電影資料館（Cinematheque Busan）純典藏
  • ACFM 線上市場放映：亞洲內容暨電影市場展 10/6–31 線上放映，僅限 2026 Market Badge 持有者，牽涉銷售策略，勾之前先跟 Fred 對齊

已定案：三項授權（媒體試看、影像圖書館、ACFM 線上放映）皆「放棄」，媒體或業界想看片，一律由公關與銷售代理個案處理。宣傳片 8/5 定案不製作、不釋出（原表單勾選由 BIFF 製作，8/5 去信改為不製作）。出處：信件 ✉8✉9

申請人與同意條款

  • 申請人資料（姓名、身分、公司、地址、Email、電話）；申請人＝出品公司或其書面授權的公司
  • 同意條款含：遵守 FIAPF 國際影展規範、承諾不撤片
Step 2演職員 Credits›

這一步填的內容會直接登上 BIFF 官網。表單原文

  • 卡司：姓名＋角色，逐一 Add
  • 主創：導演、編劇、美術、剪接、聲音（下拉選單）
  • 姓名拼寫同 Step 1：姓氏全大寫、名字首字母大寫
  • 出品公司：公司名、地址、聯絡人、Email、電話、傳真
  • World Sales 公司與韓國發行商欄位：資料要向 Fred 確認，見待確認

現況（8/10）：演職員名單、Karen YIP 服裝設計排序與版權標註「Splash Pictures, Inc」均已由大會套用，大會並表示即將關閉線上報名表；姓名定案：Ko Chin CHEN、Shuhua Yeh 均不加連字號。出處：信件 ✉12✉15

Step 3拷貝資訊 Print Info›
  • Print Owner（拷貝權利方）必填：公司、地址、聯絡人、Email
  • Print Sender／Print Address：只有實體運送才必填；可勾「同上」或「To be determined」先過關

建議補充背景：正式拷貝是數位交付還是實體寄送，等後期與大會確認規格後再定；此步先填 Owner、其餘勾 TBD，之後再回報 Program Team 更新。表單原文

Step 4必附附件 Compulsory Attachment›

劇照（主圖 #1＋劇照 #2–#5）

  • jpg 格式；檔名只能用 ISO 基本拉丁字母、不得有特殊符號
  • 不收電影海報、不收直式圖；網站顯示時長寬比可能微調
  • 300dpi 以上且高於 2100×1050 像素；單檔 10MB 內
  • 有版權要標版權人（會顯示在官網）、不加©符號；超過 5 張或上傳有問題直接聯絡 Program Team

對白本

  • 英文對白本＋原文（中文）對白本，兩份都必附，大會做韓文字幕的依據；中文片屬於原文對白本的強制名單

已解（8/3）：表單已順利送出；字幕素材（screener＋對白本）另走期限交付，見01 關鍵時程，不卡表單。出處：信件 ✉7

Step 5送出 Complete›
  • 按 Submit/Complete 才算完成註冊
  • 送出的資料用於大會出版物、官網、官方社群，送出前全員最後校一次對外文字
  • 送出後任何變動，必須直接回報 Program Team

03素材清單與規格

Materials Check-up List 白紙黑字的期限與規格。多數素材 TIFF 註冊時已備過一輪，可沿用，但要按 BIFF 的解析度與格式重新檢查。出處：素材清單

項目數量期限備註
宣傳素材 Publicity Materials：8/11（已於 8/3 隨表交齊）
線上報名表18/3 ✓已送出；等五項複查回覆後關表，見報名表拆解
英文大綱18/11報名表另分短版（45 字內）與長版，兩者長度須不同
導演簡歷18/1145 字（words）以上
導演作品年表18/11
導演照18/11清單：每張至少 1MB、300dpi 以上；表單另要求高於 1080×1440 像素、jpg
劇照（高解析）3–58/3 ✓實際僅提供 1 張，大會接受（暫不追加；若之後要補，需告知上傳時間）
宣傳片段—定案不製作、不釋出，大會已確認 OK；若日後要釋出，9 月中前交大會（上官方 YouTube＋影片頁）
字幕素材 Subtitling Materials：8/25（大會 8/18 確認，嚴格）｜8/25 交定剪檔與對白本、8/26 補中文對白本，大會確認全部到位
定剪 screener（燒英字）18/25 *畫面與聲音都要鎖定（picture and audio-locked）；用可下載連結交付（Vimeo／Google Drive／WeTransfer）
對白本（英文）18/25 *兩份都是大會做韓文字幕的必要文件；中文片必交原文版。中文版 8/26 應大會譯者要求另交
對白本（原文中文）18/25 *
放映拷貝 Screening Print：9/4
放映拷貝（燒英字）19/4放映格式「請事先詢問」；DCP 規格見報名表 Step 1

* 8/25 大會已確認（嚴格期限）：原 8/11 → 8/3 核准延至 8/19 → 8/18 大會再確認直接於 8/25 交最終定稿版（取代 8/19，不分兩次交）；韓文字幕約需一個月；大會說明 8/25 已超出官方最終期限，視同沒有逾期空間。交件標準同步提高為畫面與聲音都鎖定，8/19 那版可接受的音樂未定不再適用；調色大會未提。出處：信件 ✉6✉25

規格數字有兩套：清單寫「每張至少 1MB、300dpi」，表單寫導演照高於 1080×1440、劇照高於 2100×1050，兩套都滿足最保險補充背景。（Technical Specification Guideline 附件尚未到手，列在待確認清單）✉1

Warmest greetings on behalf of the 31st Busan International Film Festival! This is Sujin Kim from the Program Team. It is a great honor to have Happily Ever After as part of our official selection this year. I'll be your main point of contact for collecting and managing all relevant materials.

As for the first step, we kindly request the followings. We would greatly appreciate it if you could share the materials by August 3rd. Please let me know in advance in case you require more time.

1. Online Registration Form — Please fill in the Online Registration Form for materials required for our promotional activities. The information and data submitted in the form will serve as a basis for various publications. It is very important that they are in accordance with the Materials Check-up List (attached) and guidelines on the form.

  • Link: https://guest.biff.kr/submission/selection/main_eng.asp?film_number=178539307526181 · Access code: 1167
  • Sample: https://guest.biff.kr/submission/selection/sample/sample01_eng.html
  • This form is optimized for the most recent version of Chrome; the form must be completed by the appropriate applicant only; report any technical problems to filmguest@biff.kr

2. Subtitling materials — For Korean subtitling, (1) a finalized screener with burnt-in English subtitles and (2) dialogue lists in both English and the original language are required. Please share the screener via an online platform of your choice; we prefer it to be downloadable with matching specs outlined in the attached Technical Specification Guideline.

Lastly, we would like to remind you of the press embargo. The full program will be announced around early September during the press conference. Please make sure to refrain from disclosing it in any publicity regarding this selection before our official announcement.

However, Shuhua's team have been using the title 행복하고 즐거운 날들 in communications with her fanbase since April. We're concerned that having two different Korean titles creates confusion and inconsistency across publicity and communications, both for BIFF and Shuhua's agency.

Linhan → Would it be possible to update our Korean title to 행복하고 즐거운 날들 across the BIFF festival website and future related communications?

Linhan → We will also push harder with Shuhua's team to arrange for her to do the pre-screening greeting on Oct 7 premiere.

〔按：原文標示 Date: 2026年9月2日週三 上午11:40，時區未註明。同一封信一併提到會再向舒華團隊爭取她出席 10/7 首映前的致意，這件事追蹤在05 待確認清單的戶外舞台見面會條目，非本信主題。〕

✉ 44 · 2026/9/2 · Sun Young → Linhan（回絕韓文片名變更）

Sun Young → I'm a little concerned, though, that it may be too late for us to make a change at this point. If this had been shared with us earlier, we certainly could have considered it.

Sun Young → The Korean title was already announced at yesterday's press conference and included in the press kit, and all of our related publications and promotional materials have already been prepared using that title. I also referred to the film as 오래오래 행복하게 in the 더 친절한 프로그래머 video I filmed to introduce the selected films, and we have already shot short-form videos using the same title as well. Changing it now would therefore be more likely to create confusion.

Sun Young → Unless a film has a Korean distributor, we normally do not consult with the film team when deciding on the Korean title. Choosing the Korean title is part of the programmer's work, and I selected this one because I felt it suited the film best.

Sun Young → Given how far along we are in the process, I don't think it would be possible for us to change the title now.

〔按：原文標示 Sent : 2026-09-02 12:40:05（韓國時間格式）。「yesterday's press conference」指 9/1 記者會。此信答覆 ✉43 的請求，韓文片名維持大會原譯 오래오래 행복하게。附件三個：BIFF_Logo_Simple.jpg 與另兩張圖片，均為簽名檔或版面圖，未讀。〕

✉ 45 · 2026/9/2 · Linhan → Sun Young（接受韓文片名決定；確認舒華與導演將出席戶外舞台見面會）

Linhan → Although this situation has been less than ideal, we have communicated extensively with Shuhua's team and convinced them to accept BIFF's Korean title, and to change their existing communications accordingly.

Linhan → When we brought Yen and Ai-Lee to BIFF in 2024, there was clear communication with us in advance regarding the proposed Korean title. Unfortunately, we did not receive the same heads up for this film. This was particularly important given Shuhua's Korean fanbase, and that her team had already been communicating a different Korean title publicly for several months. I hope the key festival communications and decisions affecting the film's publicity can be coordinated with us in advance to avoid similar situations in the future.

Linhan → On the other hand, we have happier news: we have convinced Shuhua's team to do the extended introduction before the Oct 7 premiere. Both Director Fiona Roan and Shuhua are confirmed at the moment. We producers would also be happy to join the introduction if useful.

I would also like to share some information regarding Shuhua’s flight arrangements.

If Shuhua will be traveling from Seoul to Busan, we would be happy to provide *a round-trip economy-class flight between Seoul and Busan* as part of our hospitality support.

Please kindly understand that, unfortunately, we are unable to cover a business-class flight through the festival.

If Shuhua will instead be traveling directly from Taiwan to Busan, however, we may not be able to provide flight support from our side. In that case, we would greatly appreciate it if you could kindly discuss her flight arrangements with TAICCA.

We hope we can welcome both Shuhua and Director Fiona to Busan and make their festival experience as comfortable and enjoyable as possible.

*1.* *10/6 Opening Ceremony & Red Carpet*

We would really love to have all six team members attend the Opening Ceremony and walk the red carpet. However, access to the Opening Ceremony venue and the red carpet is limited to guests who have received an official invitation to the Opening Ceremony.

For each film team, we can accommodate up to five guests for the Opening Ceremony and red carpet. Therefore, we would greatly appreciate it if you could select five team members from the previously shared list who will attend the Opening Ceremony and walk the red carpet, and let us know their names.

We are very sorry, but team members who do not receive an Opening Ceremony invitation will not be able to enter the Opening Ceremony venue.

If the actors require staff members to accompany them, we can issue an access pass for one staff member per actor. Please note, however, that these staff members will only be able to access the waiting area and will not be permitted to enter the main ceremony hall.

There is also a designated area within the Opening Ceremony venue where photography and video recording are possible. However, access to this area is limited to selected press members who have been approved in advance. Therefore, we are afraid that your team’s photographer and videographer will not be able to take photos or videos at the event. Our official photographers will be covering the event, and we will share the official photos with you as soon as we receive them after the ceremony.

葉舒華官方邀請函BIFF 大會來函 · 2026/8/14 · PDF›
2026/8/14 · JUNG Hanseok（Festival Director）→ Ms. YEH Shuhua（正式邀請函）

Dear Ms. YEH Shuhua,

Greetings from BUSAN International Film Festival!

BUSAN International Film Festival invites you to the upcoming 31st edition, which will take place from October 6 to 15, 2026. We would be honored to have you present Happily Ever After in the A Window on Asian Cinema section. A Window on Asian Cinema section explores the vibrant creativity and storytelling of Asian cinema through exceptional works by renowned filmmakers and promising new voices, reflecting the dynamic spirit of modern Asian filmmaking.

Your presence in Busan will make the festival more meaningful and successful to the film communities here in Asia. Please kindly consider our sincere invitation to participate in the festival. It is our pleasure to provide you with a 4 nights of hotel accommodation in one of our partner hotels.

We sincerely hope that you kindly accept this invitation, and look forward to welcoming you at the Busan Cinema Center in October. Please do not hesitate to contact us if you require any further details.

Sincerely,
JUNG Hanseok · Festival Director

原檔：資料/[Busan IFF 2026] Invitation for Ms. YEH Shuhua.pdf（信函抬頭日期 2026/8/14，檔案 8/15 存入專案）。

Materials Check-up ListBIFF 信件附件 · 截圖存檔›
Materials Check-up List · For official selection of BUSAN International Film Festival

Please make sure that you provide the following materials by the due date.

Publicity Materials — August 11

  • Registration Form ×1 — Individual online link will be provided
  • English synopsis ×1
  • Director's biography ×1 — more than 45 words
  • Director's filmography ×1
  • Director's photo ×1 — minimum 1 megabyte each, high resolution more than 300 dpi required
  • High quality images of the film ×3 to 5 — 同上規格
  • Promotional Video Clip ×1 — Optional. 1–2 minutes, 1920×1080 px, .MP4 or MOV; must be a clean version without subtitles. If it takes more than mentioned deadline, please inform us in advance.

Subtitling Materials — August 11

  • Final Version of Screener (Film File), English subtitle burnt in ×1 — Must be identical to the exhibition print. Please use a downloadable link like Vimeo, Google Drive, WeTransfer etc.
  • Dialogue list in English ×1
  • Dialogue list in the Original language ×1 — Compulsory for Chinese, Japanese, French, Italian, German, Spanish

Screening Print — September 4

  • Screening print w/ English subtitle ×1 — Regarding the screening format, please inquire in advance

截圖原檔：資料/S__100802584_0.jpg

線上報名表範本（五步驟）資料/報名資料 · 5 份 PDF›
Step 1 · Film Info（規格重點摘錄）
  • English Title / Original Title / Korean romanization — please write as pronounced, in ISO basic Latin alphabet
  • Date of Production (MM/YYYY) — please enter the date when the film has been completed
  • Language(s), Subtitled, Production Country (可加多國), Running Time, Category (Fiction / Documentary / Animation / Other)
  • AI Participation: None / Partial / Full — please check if the film includes scenes partially or entirely generated by artificial intelligence
  • Premiere: World / International / Asian / Korean / Not Applicable — Asian premieres are films screened for the first time in public in Asia
  • Festival(s) and screening(s) the film has been or will be screened before the 31st BIFF (6–15 October 2026)；Prizes or Funds (if applicable)
  • Short Synopsis: no more than 45 words；Long Synopsis — Short and Long Synopsis should be in different length；both should be uploaded in the material, it will be mostly used
  • Director: Name（Last Name and the first letter of First Name should be used in capital letters）, Nationality, Gender, E-mail, Telephone
  • Director's Photo: no less than 300 dpi and higher than 1080×1440 pixels, in jpg format, max 10MB；copyright — please exclude the copyright symbol (©)
  • Biography: more than 45 words；Filmography 逐年列；可 Add another director
  • Screening Format: Unencrypted DCP（最優先）/ Encrypted DCP / Film (35mm) / HDCAM / Other — if encrypted, send DKDM to the Print Coordinator of Busan IFF
  • 2D/3D · Color/B&W · 2K/4K · FPS — (DCP) 24fps or 25fps only, other frame rates will not be able to screen
  • DCP content: 3.5" HDD or case with USB interface and external power supply；MBR partition table；NTFS, EXT2 or EXT3；burnt-in subtitles are encouraged；XML subtitle font file under 640KB；5.1 sound channel recommended
  • Promotional video clip: upload file or download link + password — if not sent, BIFF will produce a 1–2 minute clip from the clean screener
  • Festival Preview Service Permission — preview service for restricted, pre-registered press members before the festival（agree / decline）
  • Festival Video Library Permission — during the festival, restricted venues only, no duplication or loaning；after the festival the screener will be preserved at Cinematheque Busan for archival purpose only（agree / decline）
  • ACFM Online Screening Permission — operated by Asian Contents & Film Market, 6 to 31 October 2026, exclusively for 2026 ACFM attendees / Market Badge holders（agree / decline）
  • Applicant and Agreement — the applicant is either the production company lending the film or a company empowered by the production company；abide by FIAPF regulations；the applicant commits to refrain from withdrawing the film from the festival
Step 2 · Credits（規格重點摘錄）
  • The below information will be uploaded on the Busan International Film Festival Official Website
  • Cast: Name + Role；Credit: Director / Script / Production Design / Editor / Sound
  • Last Name and the first letter of First Name should be used in capital letters
  • Production Company / World Sales Company / Distribution Company for Korean Territory — 各含公司名、地址、聯絡人、E-mail、電話、傳真
Step 3 · Print Info（規格重點摘錄）
  • Print Owner — Contact Person 與 E-mail(Primary) 為必填
  • Print Sender / Print Address — Required for physical shipping only；可勾 Check if same as the print owner/sender 或 To be determined
Step 4 · Compulsory Attachment（規格重點摘錄）
  • Main image #1 + Still images #2–#5 — the attached photos are intended for use in the publication, on the official website, and across the official social media accounts
  • jpg format；the file name should contain only ISO basic Latin alphabet, with no special characters
  • Please do not include film posters or vertically oriented images；aspect ratio of the main image may be slightly adjusted when displayed
  • No less than 300 dpi and higher than 2100×1050 pixels；maximum file size 10MB；more than 5 images or upload difficulty → contact the Program Team directly
  • If any image is copyrighted, kindly specify the copyright holder（this information will be displayed on the official website）；do not include the copyright symbol (©)
  • English dialogue list + Dialogue list in the original language — both files are necessary documents for making Korean subtitles in BUSAN IFF；original language list is required for Chinese, Japanese, French, Italian, German, Spanish
Step 5 · Complete（規格重點摘錄）
  • 按 Submit / Complete 才完成註冊
  • All the information you have submitted will be used for Busan International Film Festival's publication, official website, and official social media accounts, so please make sure all data are accurate and up-to-date
  • Should there be any changes required, you must report the changes directly to Program Team
